Title: Réponses de M. l'Archevêque de Cambray à la Déclaration de M. l'Archevêque de Paris, de M. l'Évêque de Meaux et de M. l'Évêque de Chartres, et à l'ouvrage de M. de Meaux intitulé Summa, etc., contre le livre intitulé Explication des Maximes des Saints
Creator: François de Salignac de La Mothe-Fénelon, archbishop of Cambrai
Publisher: A Bruxelles, chez Eugène Henry Fricx, imprimeur du Roy
Publication Date: 1698
Rights: Public Domain (published 1698, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item's own rights statement reads 'http://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/mark/1.0/')
Language: fra
Source: Internet Archive item bub_gb_ntgdmY4cHVQC (https://archive.org/details/bub_gb_ntgdmY4cHVQC), the item's own bub_gb_ntgdmY4cHVQC_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: National Central Library of Rome; host metadata date: 1698. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: 353e0bd339d1f2d70184f9a05c61b4f77e19808ae9adc5e4ab71eb6189e9844b.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. National Central Library of Rome copy scanned by Google and contributed to the Internet Archive; good OCR, long s as f; repeated "Digitized by Google" lines are left in place.
Content note: Fénelon's point-by-point defence of the Maxims in 1698: each numbered paragraph of the three bishops' Declaration printed in turn and followed by his Réponse, then his answer to Bossuet's Latin Summa doctrinae.
